## Grok handoff — 2026-07-04 (chart-view wheel NaN fix, ap-v601)

**Bug hunt found chart-view share links throwing hundreds of SVG NaN line errors.** Root cause: `chart-view.html` passed raw `AstroEphemeris` output (`positions.sun.longitude`, lowercase keys) straight to `renderNatalChart`, which expected chart-page shape (`Sun.lon`). Fix: `normalizeChartData()` + `readLon()` in `chart-render.js` (accepts both shapes; guards aspect/planet drawing); chart-view passes `ascendant` + rising from engine. `bug-hunt.mjs` tool added. Bug hunt + npm test green. SW **ap-v601**. NOT pushed.

## Claude handoff — 2026-07-04 (homepage texture diet: WebP + mobile tier + saturn recompress + sign CLS)

**Perf pass on the WebGL hero + sign pages. Measured before/after with playwright (Fast-3G mobile 390x844 CPU4x + desktop).**
- **assets/textures/** — all planet/earth maps converted JPG/PNG→**WebP q82** (data maps earth_normal/earth_specular q90-92 + sharp-yuv to protect the HD Earth shader; saturn_ring lossless-alpha). Full-res texture set **6553KB→2585KB (-60.6%)**; earth_lights **718KB PNG→108KB WebP**. 512px `_sm.webp` variants regenerated for all (mobile set = **204KB**). Old .jpg/.png KEPT on disk (see sw.js note).
- **js/orrery-webgl.js** — texture loader now maps logical .jpg/.png names → **.webp** in `textureCandidates()` (single choke point; individual body defs untouched). New `wantsSmallTextures()` tier gate: low/mid perfTier **OR** `(pointer:coarse)`+`(max-width:820px)` **OR** innerWidth≤560 → loads `_sm.webp`; high-tier desktop → full-res. Fixes the audit's "mid-range phone reporting high-tier pulls 2.86MB" hole (that device now pulls 204KB `_sm.webp`, verified). Legacy .jpg/.png kept as last-ditch fallback so nothing 404s.
- **website/img/engine/saturn.webp** — recompressed **195KB→65KB** (the bloat was an ImageMagick lossless-alpha default; fixed via `-quality 82 -define webp:alpha-quality=64`, alpha PRESERVED). Rings/Cassini/hexagon print-grade (RMSE 0.6%, visually identical). Used as Capricorn hero.
- **website/tools/generate-sign-pages.mjs + 12 regenerated signs** — sign-hero **CLS 0.0597→0.0008** (root cause was the seal glyph reserve `4.5rem` under-reserving vs the ~112px rendered `.ap-seal`, and the `:not(:has(.ap-seal))` guard dropping the reserve once the seal mounted → whole hero column dropped ~32px). Fix: reserve the glyph box at its FINAL geometry (matches celestial-seals.css clamp) in ALL states + preload the Cormorant display woff2. **git-diff of all 12 signs = ONLY these 2 CLS hunks (27 lines), ZERO OG/hero/branding lines touched** — the OG-card agent's meta is intact.
- **Measured deltas** — Desktop homepage total **8450KB→4485KB (-47%)**, LCP 1120→956ms. Mobile total 2081→2072KB (already `_sm`; mobile weight is now JS-bound ~1120KB, out of scope). Desktop hero screenshot read: photoreal Earth (albedo+normal+specular ocean-glint+clouds+lights+atmo) all render correctly in WebP; all planets .webp 200, **0 console errors, 0 texture 404s**. Reduced-motion (chart-wheel hero) + 2D canvas fallback (orrery3d.js injected) both clean. Reports/shots in tools/visual-check/out/redesign/perf-*.
- ⚠ **sw.js (lead-owned, NOT touched by me)** — its PRECACHE still lists the old `.jpg/.png` texture paths (generated by `tools/generate-sw-precache.mjs`). I kept the originals on disk so `c.addAll` won't 404 on install; the `.webp` are served via the runtime fetch-cache. **Lead action: rerun `generate-sw-precache.mjs` (globs assets/textures → will pick up .webp) + bump V** so the WebP set is precached; ideally delete the stale .jpg/.png first so they don't get re-precached. `after_project_edit.ps1 "AstroPrecise"` run. NOT pushed. — Claude

Plus 3 overlapping product grids (~11,500px) → collapse to ONE catalogue; apparel SKUs need mockups.

## Claude handoff — 2026-07-04 (chart.html expert audit, ap-v581)

**chart.html got the same expert visual/UX treatment the homepage got.** 3-lens critique fan-out (visual design · form UX/conversion · results presentation) over desktop+mobile screenshots, then implemented. **Wheel (chart-render.js):** fixed a real honesty bug — the "ASC at nine o'clock" caption was a lie (`lonToAngle` used `180−Δ` but `polar()` is 0°=top/CW, so ASC rendered at 6 o'clock); now `270−Δ` → ASC left, MC top, IC bottom, DESC right (conventional + caption now true). Retired the whole warm `WARM{}` palette (`#050406`/`#C9A227`…) → cool tokens; element accents off the retired violet (`air #A78BBA`→`#8A7A6A`), Sun brass, aspect Square off the CTA-orange; dropped the redundant 2-letter SIGN_ABBR text (seals already label the ring). **Share cards (chart-page.js):** retired warm `PAL{}` + all `rgba(196,146,10…)`/`rgba(201,162,39…)`/oxblood literals → cool tokens (the printed/shared assets were fully off-brand). **House-system honesty (was a data bug):** the form computed `equal` but the save/share/JSON path defaulted `placidus` (`chart.houseSystem` was never set) → reopened saved charts could silently recompute different cusps. Fixed: `houseSystem` now flows onto the chart; Houses tab shows **"Equal houses — switchable"** with a live segmented switcher (Equal/Placidus/Whole Sign) that recomputes in place (reconstructs input from the chart, works for restored shares too). **Form handoff (P0):** the homepage `?date=` prefill was silent — now acknowledged inline + focus jumps to the next empty field (city/time) + honest noon/approximate note + a "Don't know your time?" affordance that opens Advanced and jumps to the time-of-day picks. Also: killed a **double-boot bug** (deferred script ran both the readyState branch and DOMContentLoaded → double-wired accordion + `restoreFromURL()`×2) via a run-once guard; removed the duplicate inline accordion handler. **Results hierarchy:** 7 near-equal action buttons → 1 primary (Save) + 3 secondary (Share/Download image/Free wallpaper) + a `<details>` "More options" (Big Three/JSON/Open in App); Rising big-three card now shows its degree (was blank); bottom email-capture no longer asserts "Unlocked your wallpaper?" when they didn't (conditional/suppressed — no double-ask). **ART-DIRECTION #3 "your sky":** added a dimmed, masked, deterministic per-birth backdrop behind the wheel (`paintWheelSky`) — a *still*, no live RAF, with an `Orrery3D.captureFrame` hook that upgrades to a real engine capture IF a live orrery is ever mounted (it isn't on this page; note: orrery3d.js has no teardown, so a hidden boot would leak a permanent RAF — deliberately not done). Verified: `npm test` green, new `tools/visual-check/chart-audit-verify.mjs` 15/15 (handoff focus, switcher recompute, rising degree, action row, sky canvas, 0 console errors), fresh screenshots eyeballed desktop+mobile. New tooling: `chart-audit-shots.mjs` + `chart-audit-verify.mjs`. **NOT pushed** (left for Jonny). SW **ap-v581**. Also done this pass: `#wallpaper-lead` moved below the wheel/tabs (reading before the email ask — DOM order verified); compat/transit `#FFD700` table headers → brass `#CDAE6A` (compatibility.html re-smoked, 0 console errors). Only remaining P2: aspect-operator unicode glyphs (no seal art exists for aspect symbols — standard notation, not planet/zodiac UI). ⚠ Shared-machine note: chart-render.js showed already-committed under v580 mid-session while my other 4 files stayed working-tree-modified — background committer is active; verify the full set lands before deploy. — Claude

## Claude handoff — 2026-07-04 (intro polish, ap-v580)

Fixed the "flashes of the old 3D model" intro bug. Root cause: #apHeroWheelFallback (engraved no-JS wheel) is a SIBLING of the orrery wrap, so it painted from frame 1 as the static fallback and stayed ~2s until the WebGL Earth replaced it. Fix: a tiny inline head script sets html.ap-await-webgl before first paint on WebGL-bound devices (capable + !PRM); critical inline CSS then hides the wheel + reveals the wrap ([hidden] override) so a calm Earth loading placeholder (#orreryEarthLoader — dark ocean sphere + atmosphere rim + brass acquiring-scan, in the v580 ap-horizon layer) shows immediately and the photoreal Earth fades in over it. Loader retires on orrery-full OR orrery-live (2D-fallback path) + a 12s safety. Reduced-motion / no-JS keep the engraved wheel (verified). Contract 20/20, npm green. SW ap-v580. Files: index.html (head script + critical rules + loader markup), js/ap-award-orrery.js (early class + markLive), css/ap-horizon-2026.css (v580 layer). — Claude

### 3. Horoscope accuracy (ap-v119)

**Problem fixed:** Old readings used static text pools with **fabricated aspects** (“Mars trine Moon”) — not computed from the sky. Violated honesty / “real positions” marketing.

**Solution:** `website/js/horoscope-engine.js` — transit-based **solar-chart** horoscope (whole-sign houses from Sun sign) using **VSOP87 ephemeris** at local noon:

- Overview: real Moon sign + solar house + lunar phase + top aspect to solar Sun (15° representative)
- Love / career / health: Venus/Mars/Moon house themes + actual positions
- `skyFacts[]`: auditable line (“Moon in X · Mercury in Y retrograde · solar house N”)
- `moodScore`: energy bar from harmonious vs challenging aspects
- Monthly: `getMonthlyHoroscope()` — Jupiter/Saturn/Sun solar-house themes from mid-month ephemeris
- **Scope honesty:** collective sun-sign reading, not full natal transits → personal chart = `transits.html`

**Wiring:**

| Page | Scripts |
|---|---|
| `horoscope.html` | `ephemeris.js` + `horoscope-engine.js` (dropped 464KB `interpretations.js` on this page) |
| All 12 sign pages | `ephemeris.js` + `horoscope-engine.js` + `sign-daily.js` (regenerated via `node website/tools/generate-sign-pages.mjs`) |
| `horoscope-subscribe.js` | Delegates monthly to engine when loaded |

**UI on horoscope panel:** `#srp-sky-facts`, `#srp-method-note`, badge “Solar-chart reading — VSOP87 sky at local noon”.
